
> 已脱敏，敏感信息用 xxx 代替


---

# 故障自愈 / 故障处理

自动化故障检测与恢复，包括进程自愈、磁盘清理、时间同步、机器替换、主机转移故障模块等。

## 【故障自愈】检查DUMP文件是否已上传COS并通知

```json
[
  {"type": "StartEvent", "id": "start", "name": ""},
  {"type": "Activity", "id": "n1", "name": "检查dump上传cos是否成功-含清理", "code": "job_execute_task"},
  {"type": "Activity", "id": "n2", "name": "定时5天", "code": "sleep_timer"},
  {"type": "Activity", "id": "n3", "name": "通知dump上传成功并且输出cos地址", "code": "remote_plugin", "plugin_code": "bkchat-sops-send", "plugin_version": "1.0.0"},
  {"type": "Activity", "id": "n4", "name": "删除完整dmp文件", "code": "job_fast_execute_script"},
  {"type": "EndEvent", "id": "end", "name": ""},
  {"type": "Link", "source": "n1", "target": "n3"},
  {"type": "Link", "source": "start", "target": "n1"},
  {"type": "Link", "source": "n3", "target": "n2"},
  {"type": "Link", "source": "n2", "target": "n4"},
  {"type": "Link", "source": "n4", "target": "end"},
  {"type": "Variable", "key": "${log_outputs}", "name": "JOB全局变量", "show_type": "hide", "source_type": "component_outputs"},
  {"type": "Variable", "key": "${server_ip}", "name": "产生DUMP文件的服务器IP"}
]
```

## 故障自愈 - 【自愈套餐】转移主机至故障机模块

```json
[
  {"type": "StartEvent", "id": "start", "name": ""},
  {"type": "Activity", "id": "n1", "name": "转移主机至故障机模块", "code": "cmdb_transfer_fault_host"},
  {"type": "EndEvent", "id": "end", "name": ""},
  {"type": "Link", "source": "start", "target": "n1"},
  {"type": "Link", "source": "n1", "target": "end"},
  {"type": "Variable", "key": "${cc_host_ip}", "name": "主机内网IP", "source_type": "component_inputs"}
]
```

## 【正式环境】xxx进程故障自愈

```json
[
  {"type": "StartEvent", "id": "start", "name": ""},
  {"type": "Activity", "id": "n1", "name": "定时", "code": "sleep_timer"},
  {"type": "Activity", "id": "n2", "name": "检查xxx进程数", "code": "job_fast_execute_script"},
  {"type": "Activity", "id": "n3", "name": "审批", "code": "bk_approve"},
  {"type": "Activity", "id": "n4", "name": "检查xxx进程数", "code": "job_fast_execute_script"},
  {"type": "Activity", "id": "n5", "name": "重置pid文件", "code": "job_fast_execute_script"},
  {"type": "Activity", "id": "n6", "name": "发送通知", "code": "bk_notify"},
  {"type": "Activity", "id": "n7", "name": "查询pid文件是否存在", "code": "job_fast_execute_script"},
  {"type": "EndEvent", "id": "end", "name": ""},
  {"type": "Link", "source": "n6", "target": "end"},
  {"type": "Link", "source": "n4", "target": "n6"},
  {"type": "Link", "source": "n1", "target": "n4"},
  {"type": "Link", "source": "start", "target": "n7"},
  {"type": "Link", "source": "n2", "target": "n3"},
  {"type": "Link", "source": "n7", "target": "n2"},
  {"type": "Link", "source": "n3", "target": "n5"},
  {"type": "Link", "source": "n5", "target": "n1"},
  {"type": "Variable", "key": "${ip}", "name": "区服IP"},
  {"type": "Variable", "key": "${log_outputs2}", "name": "JOB全局变量", "show_type": "hide", "source_type": "component_outputs"},
  {"type": "Variable", "key": "${log_outputs3}", "name": "JOB全局变量", "show_type": "hide", "source_type": "component_outputs"},
  {"type": "Variable", "key": "${log_outputs}", "name": "JOB全局变量", "show_type": "hide", "source_type": "component_outputs"},
  {"type": "Variable", "key": "${proc_name}", "name": "进程名称"},
  {"type": "Variable", "key": "${set_id}", "name": "集群id"}
]
```

# 客户端发布

独立于服务端版本发布，专门针对多端客户端包的发布流程，包括 CDN 分发、Dolphin/Puffer 渠道发布、GCloud 做包等操作。

## 【dolphin】【apk】【catalog】客户端更新-创建资源版本

```json
[
  {"type": "StartEvent", "id": "start", "name": ""},
  {"type": "Activity", "id": "n1", "name": "从本地记录获取accessid", "code": "job_execute_task"},
  {"type": "Activity", "id": "n2", "name": "从本地记录获取程序版本", "code": "job_execute_task"},
  {"type": "Activity", "id": "n3", "name": "获取资源文件", "code": "job_execute_task"},
  {"type": "Activity", "id": "n4", "name": "从本地记录获取资源是否发布", "code": "job_execute_task"},
  {"type": "Activity", "id": "n5", "name": "无需资源发布-bkchat通用消息通知", "code": "remote_plugin", "plugin_code": "bkchat-sops-send", "plugin_version": "1.0.0"},
  {"type": "Activity", "id": "n6", "name": "从本地记录获取dolphin_channel_id", "code": "job_execute_task"},
  {"type": "Activity", "id": "n7", "name": "获取待发布资源文件名", "code": "job_execute_task"},
  {"type": "Activity", "id": "n8", "name": "下载资源文件到目录", "code": "job_fast_execute_script"},
  {"type": "Activity", "id": "n9", "name": "流程完成-bkchat通用消息通知", "code": "remote_plugin", "plugin_code": "bkchat-sops-send", "plugin_version": "1.0.0"},
  {"type": "Activity", "id": "n10", "name": "从本地记录获取资源版本", "code": "job_execute_task"},
  {"type": "Activity", "id": "n11", "name": "从本地记录获取gameid", "code": "job_execute_task"},
  {"type": "Activity", "id": "n12", "name": "GCloud创建资源版本", "code": "remote_plugin", "plugin_code": "gcloud-new", "plugin_version": "1.0.0"},
  {"type": "Activity", "id": "n13", "name": "从本地记录获取accesskey", "code": "job_execute_task"},
  {"type": "ConvergeGateway", "id": "cg1", "name": ""},
  {"type": "ParallelGateway", "id": "pg1", "name": "", "converge_gateway_id": "cg2"},
  {"type": "ConvergeGateway", "id": "cg2", "name": ""},
  {"type": "ExclusiveGateway", "id": "eg1", "name": "", "conditions": [{"target": "n8", "name": "有资源版本且需要发布", "expression": "\"${log_outputs['catalog_version_cloud_publish']}\" == \"yes\""}]},
  {"type": "EndEvent", "id": "end", "name": ""},
  {"type": "Link", "source": "n4", "target": "cg2"},
  {"type": "Link", "source": "pg1", "target": "n11"},
  {"type": "Link", "source": "cg1", "target": "end"},
  {"type": "Link", "source": "eg1", "target": "n8"},
  {"type": "Link", "source": "n3", "target": "cg2"},
  {"type": "Link", "source": "n11", "target": "cg2"},
  {"type": "Link", "source": "n9", "target": "cg1"},
  {"type": "Link", "source": "pg1", "target": "n4"},
  {"type": "Link", "source": "n5", "target": "cg1"},
  {"type": "Link", "source": "pg1", "target": "n6"},
  {"type": "Link", "source": "pg1", "target": "n3"},
  {"type": "Link", "source": "n13", "target": "cg2"},
  {"type": "Link", "source": "cg2", "target": "eg1"},
  {"type": "Link", "source": "n6", "target": "cg2"},
  {"type": "Link", "source": "n2", "target": "cg2"},
  {"type": "Link", "source": "n7", "target": "n12"},
  {"type": "Link", "source": "eg1", "target": "n5"},
  {"type": "Link", "source": "n12", "target": "n9"},
  {"type": "Link", "source": "pg1", "target": "n1"},
  {"type": "Link", "source": "n8", "target": "n7"},
  {"type": "Link", "source": "pg1", "target": "n2"},
  {"type": "Link", "source": "pg1", "target": "n13"},
  {"type": "Link", "source": "pg1", "target": "n10"},
  {"type": "Link", "source": "n1", "target": "cg2"},
  {"type": "Link", "source": "n10", "target": "cg2"},
  {"type": "Link", "source": "start", "target": "pg1"},
  {"type": "Variable", "key": "${client_type}", "name": "客户端类型"},
  {"type": "Variable", "key": "${env}", "name": "环境"},
  {"type": "Variable", "key": "${log_outputs_CATALOG_FILENAME}", "name": "JOB全局变量", "show_type": "hide", "source_type": "component_outputs"},
  {"type": "Variable", "key": "${log_outputs_GCLOUD_ACCESS_ID}", "name": "JOB全局变量", "show_type": "hide", "source_type": "component_outputs"},
  {"type": "Variable", "key": "${log_outputs_GCLOUD_ACCESS_KEY}", "name": "JOB全局变量", "show_type": "hide", "source_type": "component_outputs"},
  {"type": "Variable", "key": "${log_outputs_catalog_version}", "name": "JOB全局变量", "show_type": "hide", "source_type": "component_outputs"},
  {"type": "Variable", "key": "${log_outputs_dolphin_channel_id}", "name": "JOB全局变量", "show_type": "hide", "source_type": "component_outputs"},
  {"type": "Variable", "key": "${log_outputs_gcloud_gameid}", "name": "JOB全局变量", "show_type": "hide", "source_type": "component_outputs"},
  {"type": "Variable", "key": "${log_outputs_publish_apk_version}", "name": "JOB全局变量", "show_type": "hide", "source_type": "component_outputs"},
  {"type": "Variable", "key": "${log_outputs_publish_catalog_version}", "name": "JOB全局变量", "show_type": "hide", "source_type": "component_outputs"},
  {"type": "Variable", "key": "${log_outputs}", "name": "JOB全局变量", "show_type": "hide", "source_type": "component_outputs"}
]
```

## 【职能化】【xxx】【不删档】【正式环境】【xxx服】不停服热更新

```json
[
  {"type": "StartEvent", "id": "start", "name": ""},
  {"type": "Activity", "id": "n1", "name": "FTP分发服务端版本文件", "code": "server_file_dispatch", "stage_name": "分发版本包"},
  {"type": "Activity", "id": "n2", "name": "bkchat通用消息通知", "code": "remote_plugin", "plugin_code": "bkchat-sops-send", "plugin_version": "1.0.0"},
  {"type": "Activity", "id": "n3", "name": "增量更新节点logic", "code": "job_execute_task"},
  {"type": "Activity", "id": "n4", "name": "定时", "code": "sleep_timer"},
  {"type": "EndEvent", "id": "end", "name": ""},
  {"type": "Link", "source": "n3", "target": "n2"},
  {"type": "Link", "source": "start", "target": "n1"},
  {"type": "Link", "source": "n4", "target": "n3"},
  {"type": "Link", "source": "n1", "target": "n4"},
  {"type": "Link", "source": "n2", "target": "end"},
  {"type": "Variable", "key": "${exec_time}", "name": "定时时间", "custom_type": "datetime"},
  {"type": "Variable", "key": "${filename}", "name": "更新版本包"},
  {"type": "Variable", "key": "${md5}", "name": "更新版本包MD5", "description": "更新包的MD5值"},
  {"type": "Variable", "key": "${nodelist}", "name": "nodelist", "value": {"excludes": [], "filters": [], "ip": [], "selectors": ["topo"], "separator": ",", "topo": [{"bk_inst_id": 0, "bk_obj_id": "module"}], "with_cloud_id": false}, "custom_type": "ip_selector", "show_type": "hide"},
  {"type": "Variable", "key": "${serverid}", "name": "区服ID", "value": "xxx", "description": "服务器范围, 示例：10001,20003-20005"}
]
```

```json
]
```

# 灰度发布

按批次、按分区逐步推进更新，降低发布风险。常见模式包括分批灰度、指定服灰度等。

## 【职能化】【不停服】【正式服】灰度发布

```json
[
  {"type": "StartEvent", "id": "start", "name": ""},
  {"type": "Activity", "id": "n1", "name": "【重启】pvpsvr", "code": "tcm_execute_task", "stage_name": "【重启】进程"},
  {"type": "Activity", "id": "n2", "name": "【重载】pvpsvr", "code": "tcm_execute_task", "stage_name": "【重载】进程"},
  {"type": "Activity", "id": "n3", "name": "【重载】matchsvr", "code": "tcm_execute_task", "stage_name": "【重载】进程"},
  {"type": "Activity", "id": "n4", "name": "【重载】unionsvr", "code": "tcm_execute_task", "stage_name": "【重载】进程"},
  {"type": "Activity", "id": "n5", "name": "【停启】worldsvr", "code": "tcm_execute_task", "stage_name": "【重启】进程"},
  {"type": "Activity", "id": "n6", "name": "【重启】unionsvr", "code": "tcm_execute_task", "stage_name": "【重启】进程"},
  {"type": "Activity", "id": "n7", "name": "【重启】matchsvr", "code": "tcm_execute_task", "stage_name": "【重启】进程"},
  {"type": "Activity", "id": "n8", "name": "【重载】gamesvr", "code": "tcm_execute_task", "stage_name": "【重载】进程"},
  {"type": "Activity", "id": "n9", "name": "【重启】gamesvr", "code": "tcm_execute_task", "stage_name": "【重启】进程"},
  {"type": "Activity", "id": "n10", "name": "给服务质量用", "code": "remote_plugin", "stage_name": "将标准运维单据创建到ITSM", "plugin_code": "ticket-to-itsm", "plugin_version": "1.0.0"},
  {"type": "EndEvent", "id": "end", "name": ""},
  {"type": "Link", "source": "n4", "target": "n9"},
  {"type": "Link", "source": "n2", "target": "n3"},
  {"type": "Link", "source": "n6", "target": "n5"},
  {"type": "Link", "source": "n9", "target": "n1"},
  {"type": "Link", "source": "n7", "target": "n6"},
  {"type": "Link", "source": "start", "target": "n8"},
  {"type": "Link", "source": "n1", "target": "n7"},
  {"type": "Link", "source": "n3", "target": "n4"},
  {"type": "Link", "source": "n8", "target": "n2"},
  {"type": "Link", "source": "n10", "target": "end"},
  {"type": "Link", "source": "n5", "target": "n10"},
  {"type": "Variable", "key": "${SetChnName_LIST}", "name": "SET中文名", "value": "${_v3_set_group.flat__bk_chn_name}", "show_type": "hide"},
  {"type": "Variable", "key": "${SetName_LIST}", "name": "发布范围", "value": "${_v3_set_group.flat__bk_set_name}", "show_type": "hide"},
  {"type": "Variable", "key": "${WORLD_ID_LIST}", "name": "缩容模块", "value": "${_v3_set_group.flat__bk_world_id}", "show_type": "hide"},
  {"type": "Variable", "key": "${_v3_scene_set}", "name": "场景set", "value": "${SetName_LIST}", "show_type": "hide"},
  {"type": "Variable", "key": "${game_world}", "name": "Gamesvr灰度WorldID", "description": "ch1:1,ch2:2,ch3:3,ch4:4"},
  {"type": "Variable", "key": "${game_zone}", "name": "Gamesvr灰度ZoneID", "description": "小服ZoneID"},
  {"type": "Variable", "key": "${match_world}", "name": "Matchsvr灰度WorldID", "description": "ch1:1,ch2:2,ch3:3,ch4:4"},
  {"type": "Variable", "key": "${match_zone}", "name": "Matchsvr灰度ZoneID", "description": "Matchsvr区ID"},
  {"type": "Variable", "key": "${pvp_world}", "name": "PvPsvr灰度WorldID", "description": "ch1:1,ch2:2,ch3:3,ch4:4"},
```

---

# CDN / 文件分发

CDN 文件发布与预热、版本包分发、COS 对象存储上传下载等。

## CDN刷新

```json
[
  {"type": "StartEvent", "id": "start", "name": ""},
  {"type": "Activity", "id": "n1", "name": "CDN刷新插件", "code": "remote_plugin", "plugin_code": "cdn-refresh", "plugin_version": "1.0.0"},
  {"type": "EndEvent", "id": "end", "name": ""},
  {"type": "Link", "source": "start", "target": "n1"},
  {"type": "Link", "source": "n1", "target": "end"},
  {"type": "Variable", "key": "${cdn_url}", "name": "cdn待刷新url", "source_type": "component_inputs"}
]
```

## 【预发布和正式环境】cdn资源包上传_ai

```json
[
  {"type": "StartEvent", "id": "start", "name": ""},
  {"type": "Activity", "id": "n1", "name": "拉取文件", "code": "job_fast_execute_script"},
  {"type": "Activity", "id": "n2", "name": "判断客户端文件是否分发到服务器", "code": "job_fast_execute_script"},
  {"type": "Activity", "id": "n3", "name": "coscmd上传", "code": "job_execute_task"},
  {"type": "ConvergeGateway", "id": "cg1", "name": ""},
  {"type": "ExclusiveGateway", "id": "eg1", "name": "", "conditions": [{"target": "n1", "name": "文件不存在", "expression": "'${log_outputs[\"file_exist\"]}' == \"0\""}, {"target": "cg1", "name": "文件存在", "expression": "'${log_outputs[\"file_exist\"]}' == \"1\""}]},
  {"type": "EndEvent", "id": "end", "name": ""},
  {"type": "Link", "source": "cg1", "target": "n3"},
  {"type": "Link", "source": "n2", "target": "eg1"},
  {"type": "Link", "source": "start", "target": "n2"},
  {"type": "Link", "source": "n1", "target": "cg1"},
  {"type": "Link", "source": "eg1", "target": "n1"},
  {"type": "Link", "source": "n3", "target": "end"},
  {"type": "Link", "source": "eg1", "target": "cg1"},
  {"type": "Variable", "key": "${client_remote_dir}", "name": "客户端远程目录", "description": "[\n    {\"text\": \"pre\",        \"value\": \"pre\"},\n    {\"text\": \"pre2\",      \"value\": \"pre2\"},\n    {\"text\": \"prod\",       \"value\": \"prod\"}\n]"},
  {"type": "Variable", "key": "${file_name}", "name": "文件名称", "value": "${ftp_file_name}", "show_type": "hide"},
  {"type": "Variable", "key": "${ftp_file_name}", "name": "静态资源文件", "custom_type": "textarea"},
  {"type": "Variable", "key": "${log_outputs}", "name": "JOB全局变量", "show_type": "hide", "source_type": "component_outputs"},
  {"type": "Variable", "key": "${server_remote_dir}", "name": "服务端的远程目录", "description": "[\n    {\"text\": \"pre\",        \"value\": \"pre\"},\n    {\"text\": \"pre2\",      \"value\": \"pre2\"},\n    {\"text\": \"prod\",       \"value\": \"prod\"}\n]"}
]
```

```json
  {"type": "Activity", "id": "n14", "name": "用户信息二次确认", "code": "bk_http_request", "stage_name": "STEP-7"},

  {"type": "ConvergeGateway", "id": "cg1", "name": ""},
  {"type": "ExclusiveGateway", "id": "eg1", "name": "", "conditions": [{"target": "n21", "name": "审批拒绝", "expression": "${approve_result['Response']['status']} != 1"}, {"target": "n10", "name": "审批通过", "expression": "${approve_result['Response']['status']} == 1"}]},
  {"type": "ExclusiveGateway", "id": "eg2", "name": "", "conditions": [{"target": "n18", "name": "无需二次复核", "expression": "${ruleType['Response']['status']} == 2"}, {"target": "n8", "name": "内容&原子", "expression": "${ruleType['Response']['status']} == 1"}]},
  {"type": "ExclusiveGateway", "id": "eg3", "name": "", "conditions": [{"target": "n13", "name": "运营商(中)", "expression": "${banType['Response']['status']} == 4"}, {"target": "n2", "name": "直接封禁", "expression": "${banType['Response']['status']} == 8"}, {"target": "n3", "name": "运营商(大)", "expression": "${banType['Response']['status']} == 5"}, {"target": "n22", "name": "用户举报", "expression": "${banType['Response']['status']} == 7"}, {"target": "cg1", "name": "初审合规", "expression": "${banType['Response']['status']} == 1"}, {"target": "n23", "name": "内部发现", "expression": "${banType['Response']['status']} == 6"}, {"target": "n16", "name": "链接封禁", "expression": "${banType['Response']['status']} == 2"}, {"target": "n17", "name": "甲方通报", "expression": "${banType['Response']['status']} == 3"}]},
  {"type": "ExclusiveGateway", "id": "eg4", "name": "", "conditions": [{"target": "n10", "name": "不需要审批", "expression": "${data['Response']['Status']} != 1"}, {"target": "n15", "name": "需要审批", "expression": "${data['Response']['Status']} == 1"}]},
  {"type": "ExclusiveGateway", "id": "eg5", "name": "", "conditions": [{"target": "cg1", "name": "合规", "expression": "${reBanType2['Response']['status']} == 1"}, {"target": "n14", "name": "复审违规", "expression": "${reBanType2['Response']['status']} != 1"}]},
  {"type": "ExclusiveGateway", "id": "eg6", "name": "", "conditions": [{"target": "n4", "name": "复审违规", "expression": "${reBanType3['Response']['status']} != 1"}, {"target": "cg1", "name": "合规", "expression": "${reBanType3['Response']['status']} == 1"}]},
  {"type": "ExclusiveGateway", "id": "eg7", "name": "", "conditions": [{"target": "cg1", "name": "合规", "expression": "${reBanType['Response']['status']} == 1"}, {"target": "n1", "name": "复审违规", "expression": "${reBanType['Response']['status']} != 1"}]},
  {"type": "EndEvent", "id": "end", "name": ""},

]
```

# GCloud 游戏发布

基于 GCloud 平台的游戏发布与版本管理，包括正式发布、预发布、资源包上传、开区审批等。

## 【子流程】Gcloud创建区服信息-区服管理使用

```json
[
  {"type": "StartEvent", "id": "start", "name": ""},
  {"type": "Activity", "id": "n1", "name": "生成json文本", "code": "remote_plugin", "plugin_code": "tcompose-exec", "plugin_version": "1.1.0"},
  {"type": "Activity", "id": "n2", "name": "暂停确认是否需要发布gcloud", "code": "pause_node"},
  {"type": "Activity", "id": "n3", "name": "人工确认(暂停)", "code": "pause_node"},
  {"type": "Activity", "id": "n4", "name": "批量标准运维任务-执行", "code": "remote_plugin", "plugin_code": "exec-multi-task", "plugin_version": "2.0.0"},
  {"type": "Activity", "id": "n5", "name": "批量标准运维任务-创建", "code": "remote_plugin", "plugin_code": "create-muti-task", "plugin_version": "1.0.0"},
  {"type": "EndEvent", "id": "end", "name": ""},
  {"type": "Link", "source": "start", "target": "n1"},
  {"type": "Link", "source": "n1", "target": "n5"},
  {"type": "Link", "source": "n3", "target": "n4"},
  {"type": "Link", "source": "n4", "target": "n2"},
  {"type": "Link", "source": "n5", "target": "n3"},
  {"type": "Link", "source": "n2", "target": "end"},
  {"type": "Variable", "key": "${gcloud_access_id}", "name": "Gcloud-访问账号", "value": "xxx", "show_type": "hide"},
  {"type": "Variable", "key": "${gcloud_access_key}", "name": "Gcloud-访问密码", "value": "xxx", "show_type": "hide"},
  {"type": "Variable", "key": "${gcloud_game_id}", "name": "Gcloud-游戏ID", "value": "xxx", "show_type": "hide"},
  {"type": "Variable", "key": "${result_vars}", "name": "结果变量", "show_type": "hide", "source_type": "component_outputs"},
  {"type": "Variable", "key": "${set_info}", "name": "小区节点信息"},
  {"type": "Variable", "key": "${tasks_id}", "name": "任务ID", "show_type": "hide", "source_type": "component_outputs"},
  {"type": "Variable", "key": "${template_id}", "name": "创建小服的模版id", "value": "xxx", "show_type": "hide"}
]
```

## 【运维工具】现网gcloud区服导航一键开区或关闭维护

```json
[
  {"type": "StartEvent", "id": "start", "name": ""},
  {"type": "Activity", "id": "n1", "name": "发布大区", "code": "gcloud_publish"},
  {"type": "Activity", "id": "n2", "name": "CC脚本获取已对外小区集群ID", "code": "job_fast_execute_script"},
  {"type": "Activity", "id": "n3", "name": "等待执行间隔", "code": "sleep_timer"},
  {"type": "Activity", "id": "n4", "name": "发送通知", "code": "bk_notify"},
  {"type": "Activity", "id": "n5", "name": "批量更新区服状态-微信安卓-全区-维护", "code": "gcloud_batch_update_node_flag"},
  {"type": "Activity", "id": "n6", "name": "发送通知", "code": "bk_notify"},
  {"type": "Activity", "id": "n7", "name": "批量更新区服状态-微信安卓-全区-繁忙", "code": "gcloud_batch_update_node_flag"},
  {"type": "Activity", "id": "n8", "name": "批量更新区服状态-微信安卓-新区-自动", "code": "gcloud_batch_update_node_flag"},
  {"type": "Activity", "id": "n9", "name": "预发布大区", "code": "gcloud_pre_publish"},
  {"type": "Activity", "id": "n10", "name": "人工确认参数渲染是否正确", "code": "pause_node"},
  {"type": "ConvergeGateway", "id": "cg1", "name": ""},
  {"type": "ExclusiveGateway", "id": "eg1", "name": "", "conditions": [{"target": "end", "name": "循环执行完成", "expression": "${_loop} >= ${channel_id_count}"}, {"target": "n3", "name": "执行下一个大区ID", "expression": "${_loop} < ${channel_id_count}"}]},
  {"type": "ExclusiveGateway", "id": "eg2", "name": "", "conditions": [{"target": "n7", "name": "对外前", "expression": "\"${opt_type}\" == \"对外前\""}, {"target": "n5", "name": "停服前", "expression": "\"${opt_type}\" == \"停服前\""}]},
  {"type": "EndEvent", "id": "end", "name": ""},
  {"type": "Link", "source": "start", "target": "n2"},
  {"type": "Link", "source": "n10", "target": "n3"},
  {"type": "Link", "source": "n9", "target": "n1"},
  {"type": "Link", "source": "n3", "target": "eg2"},
  {"type": "Link", "source": "eg2", "target": "n7"},
  {"type": "Link", "source": "cg1", "target": "n9"},
  {"type": "Link", "source": "eg1", "target": "end"},
  {"type": "Link", "source": "n5", "target": "n4"},
  {"type": "Link", "source": "n4", "target": "cg1"},
  {"type": "Link", "source": "n8", "target": "n6"},
  {"type": "Link", "source": "n7", "target": "n8"},
  {"type": "Link", "source": "n6", "target": "cg1"},
  {"type": "Link", "source": "eg2", "target": "n5"},
  {"type": "Link", "source": "n2", "target": "n10"},
  {"type": "Link", "source": "eg1", "target": "n3"},
  {"type": "Link", "source": "n1", "target": "eg1"},
  {"type": "Variable", "key": "${_loop}", "name": "循环次数", "show_type": "hide", "source_type": "component_outputs"},
  {"type": "Variable", "key": "${bk_app_code}", "name": "bk应用账户", "value": "xxx", "show_type": "hide"},
  {"type": "Variable", "key": "${bk_app_secret}", "name": "bk应用密钥", "value": "xxx", "custom_type": "textarea", "show_type": "hide"},
  {"type": "Variable", "key": "${bk_username}", "name": "bk应用执行人", "value": "xxx", "show_type": "hide"},
  {"type": "Variable", "key": "${channel_id_count}", "name": "大区ID个数", "value": "4", "show_type": "hide"},
  {"type": "Variable", "key": "${channel_id_list}", "name": "大区ID列表", "value": "xxx", "description": "注意大区ID数量和${channel_id_count}的值一致", "show_type": "hide"},
  {"type": "Variable", "key": "${gcloud_access_id}", "name": "Gcloud-访问账号", "value": "xxx", "show_type": "hide"},
  {"type": "Variable", "key": "${gcloud_access_key}", "name": "Gcloud-访问密码", "value": "xxx", "custom_type": "password", "show_type": "hide"},
  {"type": "Variable", "key": "${gcloud_game_id}", "name": "Gcloud-游戏ID", "value": "xxx", "show_type": "hide"},
  {"type": "Variable", "key": "${last_qq_server_id}", "name": "qq待开服小区", "value": "${log_outputs['last_qq_server_id']}", "show_type": "hide"},
  {"type": "Variable", "key": "${last_wx_server_id}", "name": "wx待开服小区", "value": "${log_outputs['last_wx_server_id']}", "show_type": "hide"},
  {"type": "Variable", "key": "${log_outputs}", "name": "JOB全局变量", "show_type": "hide", "source_type": "component_outputs"},
  {"type": "Variable", "key": "${opt_type}", "name": "区服导航设置场景", "value": {"datasource": "0", "default": "", "items_text": "[{\"text\":\"停服前\",\"value\":\"停服前\"},{\"text\":\"对外前\",\"value\":\"对外前\"}]", "type": "0"}, "custom_type": "select"},
  {"type": "Variable", "key": "${sesion_id}", "name": "BOT会话ID", "value": "xxx\nxxx", "description": "标准运维V3会话ID", "custom_type": "textarea", "show_type": "hide"},
  {"type": "Variable", "key": "${yw_ip}", "name": "运维机", "value": {"excludes": [], "filters": [], "ip": [], "selectors": ["topo"], "separator": ",", "topo": [{"bk_inst_id": 0, "bk_obj_id": "module"}], "with_cloud_id": false}, "custom_type": "ip_selector", "show_type": "hide"}
]
```

## 【预发布环境】停机更新全服_NEW_clone

```json
[
  {"type": "StartEvent", "id": "start", "name": ""},

  {"type": "EndEvent", "id": "end", "name": ""},
  {"type": "Link", "source": "n12", "target": "n1"},
```

# 合服 / 开新服 / 开区

```json
]
```

```json
[
  {"type": "StartEvent", "id": "start", "name": ""},

]
```

## 【通用】DBM-SQL变更

```json
[
  {"type": "StartEvent", "id": "start", "name": ""},
  {"type": "Activity", "id": "n1", "name": "创建目标目录", "code": "job_fast_execute_script"},
  {"type": "Activity", "id": "n2", "name": "蓝盾仓库分发", "code": "remote_plugin", "plugin_code": "devops-depot", "plugin_version": "1.0.0"},
  {"type": "Activity", "id": "n3", "name": "DBM-确认执行或终止单据", "code": "remote_plugin", "plugin_code": "bkdbm-confirm", "plugin_version": "1.4.0"},
  {"type": "Activity", "id": "n4", "name": "DBM-确认执行或终止单据", "code": "remote_plugin", "plugin_code": "bkdbm-confirm", "plugin_version": "1.4.0"},
  {"type": "Activity", "id": "n5", "name": "DBM-MySQL备份", "code": "remote_plugin", "plugin_code": "bkdbm-mysqlbu", "plugin_version": "1.2.0"},
  {"type": "Activity", "id": "n6", "name": "DBM-SQL变更", "code": "remote_plugin", "plugin_code": "bkdbm-sqlexecute", "plugin_version": "1.3.0"},
  {"type": "ExclusiveGateway", "id": "eg1", "name": "", "conditions": [{"target": "n5", "name": "备份", "expression": "${if_back} == 1"}, {"target": "n6", "name": "不备份", "expression": "${if_back} == 0"}]},
  {"type": "EndEvent", "id": "end", "name": ""},
  {"type": "Link", "source": "n1", "target": "n2"},
  {"type": "Link", "source": "n5", "target": "n3"},
  {"type": "Link", "source": "eg1", "target": "n5"},
  {"type": "Link", "source": "n3", "target": "n6"},
  {"type": "Link", "source": "n6", "target": "n4"},
  {"type": "Link", "source": "eg1", "target": "n6"},
  {"type": "Link", "source": "start", "target": "n1"},
  {"type": "Link", "source": "n4", "target": "end"},
  {"type": "Link", "source": "n2", "target": "eg1"},
  {"type": "Variable", "key": "${back_ticket_id}", "name": "单据id", "show_type": "hide", "source_type": "component_outputs"},
  {"type": "Variable", "key": "${cluster}", "name": "集群域名", "source_type": "component_inputs"},
  {"type": "Variable", "key": "${db_name}", "name": "变更DB名称"},
  {"type": "Variable", "key": "${followers}", "name": "单据关注人", "source_type": "component_inputs"},
  {"type": "Variable", "key": "${if_back}", "name": "是否备份（0：不备份，1：备份）", "value": 1, "custom_type": "int"},
  {"type": "Variable", "key": "${sql_file_name}", "name": "sql_file_name", "value": "${sql_path.split(\"/\")[-1]}", "show_type": "hide"},
  {"type": "Variable", "key": "${sql_md5}", "name": "sql文件MD5值"},
  {"type": "Variable", "key": "${sql_path}", "name": "sql文件制品库路径"},
  {"type": "Variable", "key": "${ticket_id}", "name": "单据id", "show_type": "hide", "source_type": "component_outputs"}
]
```

# 进程管理与重启

对游戏各类服务进程的启停、重启操作，常见模式包括一半一半重启、分批次重启等。

## 【跨区】cross_router一半一半重启

```json
[
  {"type": "StartEvent", "id": "start", "name": ""},

  {"type": "Activity", "id": "n26", "name": "设置router  disable=false", "code": "pause_node", "stage_name": "替换tcm配置"},

  {"type": "Variable", "key": "${before_id}", "name": "before_id", "value": "[1-15]"},

]
```

## 【提审服】重启所有进程

```json
[
  {"type": "StartEvent", "id": "start", "name": ""},
  {"type": "Activity", "id": "n1", "name": "重启", "code": "gsekit_job_exec"},
  {"type": "Activity", "id": "n2", "name": "告警屏蔽", "code": "tnm2_alarm_shield"},
  {"type": "Activity", "id": "n3", "name": "解除告警屏蔽", "code": "monitor_alarm_shield_disable"},
  {"type": "Activity", "id": "n4", "name": "蓝鲸监控告警屏蔽(按范围)", "code": "monitor_alarm_shield"},
  {"type": "Activity", "id": "n5", "name": "发送消息", "code": "wechat_work_send_message"},
  {"type": "Activity", "id": "n6", "name": "延时10分钟", "code": "sleep_timer"},
  {"type": "Activity", "id": "n7", "name": "解除告警屏蔽", "code": "tnm2_alarm_unshield"},
  {"type": "EndEvent", "id": "end", "name": ""},
  {"type": "Link", "source": "n4", "target": "n2"},
  {"type": "Link", "source": "start", "target": "n4"},
  {"type": "Link", "source": "n3", "target": "n7"},
  {"type": "Link", "source": "n5", "target": "n6"},
  {"type": "Link", "source": "n7", "target": "end"},
  {"type": "Link", "source": "n2", "target": "n1"},
  {"type": "Link", "source": "n6", "target": "n3"},
  {"type": "Link", "source": "n1", "target": "n5"},
  {"type": "Variable", "key": "${ip}", "name": "ip", "value": {"var_filter_module": "", "var_filter_set": "", "var_ip_custom_value": "", "var_ip_manual_value": {"var_manual_module": "xxx_gamesvr", "var_manual_set": "${set_id}", "var_module_name": ""}, "var_ip_method": "manual", "var_ip_select_value": {"var_module": [], "var_module_name": "", "var_set": []}}, "description": "故障ip", "custom_type": "set_module_ip_selector", "show_type": "hide"},
  {"type": "Variable", "key": "${modules}", "name": "模块名", "description": "模块名", "show_type": "hide"},
  {"type": "Variable", "key": "${set_id}", "name": "set id", "value": "3999,4999", "description": "集群ID", "show_type": "hide"},
  {"type": "Variable", "key": "${shield_id}", "name": "屏蔽Id", "show_type": "hide", "source_type": "component_outputs"},
  {"type": "Variable", "key": "${tnm2_id}", "name": "屏蔽ID", "show_type": "hide", "source_type": "component_outputs"},
  {"type": "Variable", "key": "${wechat_work_chat_id}", "name": "会话ID", "value": "xxx", "show_type": "hide", "source_type": "component_inputs"}
]
```

# 扩缩容

根据业务负载动态调整服务器资源，包括 CVM 升降配、一键扩容/缩容等。

## 【扩容】分组ch2-lobby一键扩容

```json
[
```

# CLB / 负载均衡管理

CLB 规则管理，包括 RS 上下线、权重调整、端口管理、规则查询与备份等。

## 【地域B】【同地域】【UDP】权重调整

```json
[
  {"type": "StartEvent", "id": "start", "name": ""},
  {"type": "Activity", "id": "n1", "name": "根据VIP查询负载均衡实例ID", "code": "remote_plugin", "plugin_code": "get-id-by-vips", "plugin_version": "1.0.0"},
  {"type": "Activity", "id": "n2", "name": "调整RS权重-TCPUDP", "code": "remote_plugin", "plugin_code": "hcm-weight-four", "plugin_version": "1.0.0"},
  {"type": "Activity", "id": "n3", "name": "输出本次参数", "code": "job_execute_task"},
  {"type": "Activity", "id": "n4", "name": "存在未查询到ID的VIP报错", "code": "job_fast_execute_script"},
  {"type": "ExclusiveGateway", "id": "eg1", "name": "", "conditions": [{"target": "n3", "name": "查询成功", "expression": "\"${no_match}\" == \"\""}, {"target": "n4", "name": "存在未查询成功", "expression": "\"${no_match}\" != \"\""}]},
  {"type": "ConvergeGateway", "id": "cg1", "name": ""},
  {"type": "EndEvent", "id": "end", "name": ""},
  {"type": "Link", "source": "eg1", "target": "n3"},
  {"type": "Link", "source": "n4", "target": "cg1"},
  {"type": "Link", "source": "n2", "target": "cg1"},
  {"type": "Link", "source": "n1", "target": "eg1"},
  {"type": "Link", "source": "eg1", "target": "n4"},
  {"type": "Link", "source": "start", "target": "n1"},
  {"type": "Link", "source": "n3", "target": "n2"},
  {"type": "Link", "source": "cg1", "target": "end"},
  {"type": "Variable", "key": "${_loop}", "name": "循环次数", "show_type": "hide", "source_type": "component_outputs"},
  {"type": "Variable", "key": "${_vip}", "name": "VIP列表(逗号分隔)"},
  {"type": "Variable", "key": "${match_balanceid}", "name": "查询到的ID", "show_type": "hide", "source_type": "component_outputs"},
  {"type": "Variable", "key": "${new_weight}", "name": "新权重"},
  {"type": "Variable", "key": "${no_match}", "name": "未查询到ID的VIP", "show_type": "hide", "source_type": "component_outputs"},
  {"type": "Variable", "key": "${origin_weight}", "name": "原权重"},
  {"type": "Variable", "key": "${rport}", "name": "服务器端口", "value": "8067"},
  {"type": "Variable", "key": "${rs_ip}", "name": "后端服务器", "value": {"excludes": [], "filters": [], "ip": [], "selectors": ["manual"], "separator": ",", "topo": [], "with_cloud_id": false}, "custom_type": "ip_selector"},
  {"type": "Variable", "key": "${vport}", "name": "VIP端口", "value": "18001"}
]
```

```json
[
  {"type": "StartEvent", "id": "start", "name": ""},
  {"type": "Activity", "id": "n1", "name": "刷新完成通知", "code": "wechat_work_send_message", "stage_name": "消息"},
  {"type": "Activity", "id": "n2", "name": "pushcfg *.[1-124,126].*.*", "code": "job_execute_task", "stage_name": "推送配置"},

  {"type": "ConvergeGateway", "id": "cg2", "name": ""},

  {"type": "Variable", "key": "${job_inst_url}", "name": "JOB任务链接", "show_type": "hide", "source_type": "component_outputs"},

", "show_type": "hide"},

]
```

```json
[
  {"type": "StartEvent", "id": "start", "name": ""},
```

```json
[
  {"type": "StartEvent", "id": "start", "name": ""},
  {"type": "Activity", "id": "n1", "name": "昨天2小时的在线数据", "code": "remote_plugin", "plugin_code": "bkbase-query", "plugin_version": "1.0.0"},
  {"type": "Activity", "id": "n2", "name": "上周2小时的在线数据", "code": "remote_plugin", "plugin_code": "bkbase-query", "plugin_version": "1.0.0"},

  {"type": "Activity", "id": "n4", "name": "在线分析", "code": "remote_plugin", "plugin_code": "ai-cf-online", "plugin_version": "1.0.0assistant"},
  {"type": "Activity", "id": "n5", "name": "最近2小时的在线数据", "code": "remote_plugin", "plugin_code": "bkbase-query", "plugin_version": "1.0.0"},
  {"type": "ParallelGateway", "id": "pg1", "name": "", "converge_gateway_id": "cg1"},
  {"type": "ConvergeGateway", "id": "cg1", "name": ""},
  {"type": "ExclusiveGateway", "id": "eg1", "name": "", "conditions": [{"target": "n3", "name": "需关注", "expression": "\"🟡关注\" in \"${output}\" or \"🔴异常\" in \"${output}\""}, {"target": "end", "name": "正常", "expression": "\"✅正常\" in \"${output}\""}]},
  {"type": "EndEvent", "id": "end", "name": ""},
  {"type": "Link", "source": "pg1", "target": "n2"},
  {"type": "Link", "source": "start", "target": "pg1"},
  {"type": "Link", "source": "pg1", "target": "n5"},
  {"type": "Link", "source": "n2", "target": "cg1"},
  {"type": "Link", "source": "n1", "target": "cg1"},
  {"type": "Link", "source": "pg1", "target": "n1"},
  {"type": "Link", "source": "n3", "target": "end"},
  {"type": "Link", "source": "cg1", "target": "n4"},
  {"type": "Link", "source": "n5", "target": "cg1"},
  {"type": "Link", "source": "eg1", "target": "n3"},
  {"type": "Link", "source": "eg1", "target": "end"},
  {"type": "Link", "source": "n4", "target": "eg1"},
  {"type": "Variable", "key": "${end_week}", "name": "end_week", "value": "${(datetime.datetime.strptime(now_time, fmt) - datetime.timedelta(weeks=1)).strftime(fmt)}"},
  {"type": "Variable", "key": "${end_yesterday}", "name": "end_yesterday", "value": "${(datetime.datetime.strptime(now_time, fmt) - datetime.timedelta(days=1)).strftime(fmt)}"},
  {"type": "Variable", "key": "${fmt}", "name": "fmt", "value": "\"%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S\""},
  {"type": "Variable", "key": "${now_time}", "name": "now_time", "value": "${(datetime.datetime.now() - datetime.timedelta(minutes=1)).strftime(fmt)}"},
  {"type": "Variable", "key": "${output}", "name": "Output", "show_type": "hide", "source_type": "component_outputs"},
  {"type": "Variable", "key": "${result_data}", "name": "查询结果(object)", "show_type": "hide", "source_type": "component_outputs"},
  {"type": "Variable", "key": "${start_2h}", "name": "start_2h", "value": "${(datetime.datetime.strptime(now_time, fmt) - datetime.timedelta(hours=2)).strftime(fmt)}"},
  {"type": "Variable", "key": "${start_week_2h}", "name": "start_week_2h", "value": "${(datetime.datetime.strptime(now_time, fmt) - datetime.timedelta(weeks=1, hours=2)).strftime(fmt)}"},
  {"type": "Variable", "key": "${start_yesterday_2h}", "name": "start_yesterday_2h", "value": "${(datetime.datetime.strptime(now_time, fmt) - datetime.timedelta(days=1, hours=2)).strftime(fmt)}"},
  {"type": "Variable", "key": "${week_result_data}", "name": "查询结果(object)", "show_type": "hide", "source_type": "component_outputs"},
  {"type": "Variable", "key": "${yesterday_result_data}", "name": "查询结果(object)", "show_type": "hide", "source_type": "component_outputs"}
]
```

```json
  {"type": "EndEvent", "id": "end", "name": ""},
```

```json
  {"type": "Link", "source": "n3", "target": "cg1"},
```

```json
  {"type": "Link", "source": "n8", "target": "end"},

  {"type": "Variable", "key": "${machine_type_lists}", "name": "目标机型", "value": "S5.2XLARGE16", "show_type": "hide", "source_type": "component_inputs"},

]
```

# 白名单管理

服务器/账号白名单的增删管理，用于灰度测试、内部体验、安全访问控制等。

## 【登录】白名单_兼容上传文件_clone

```json
[
  {"type": "StartEvent", "id": "start", "name": ""},
  {"type": "Activity", "id": "n1", "name": "上传本地白名单txt文件", "code": "job_push_local_files"},
  {"type": "Activity", "id": "n2", "name": "上传本地文件形式取消登录白名单", "code": "job_execute_task"},
  {"type": "Activity", "id": "n3", "name": "上传到服务器", "code": "job_local_content_upload"},
  {"type": "Activity", "id": "n4", "name": "重载当前时间戳", "code": "job_fast_execute_script"},
  {"type": "Activity", "id": "n5", "name": "激活登录白名单", "code": "job_execute_task"},
  {"type": "Activity", "id": "n6", "name": "取消登录白名单", "code": "job_execute_task"},
  {"type": "Activity", "id": "n7", "name": "上传本地文件形式激活登录白名单", "code": "job_execute_task"},
  {"type": "ExclusiveGateway", "id": "eg1", "name": "", "conditions": [{"target": "n3", "name": "【文本框填写】白名单openid", "expression": "\"${white_file_upload_type}\" == \"【文本框填写】白名单openid\""}, {"target": "n1", "name": "【本地文件上传】白名单txt文件", "expression": "\"${white_file_upload_type}\" == \"【本地文件上传】白名单txt文件\""}]},
  {"type": "ConvergeGateway", "id": "cg1", "name": ""},
  {"type": "ConvergeGateway", "id": "cg2", "name": ""},
  {"type": "ExclusiveGateway", "id": "eg2", "name": "", "conditions": [{"target": "n6", "name": "取消", "expression": "\"${oper_type}\" == \"cancel_white.txt\""}, {"target": "n5", "name": "激活", "expression": "\"${oper_type}\" == \"white.txt\""}]},
  {"type": "ExclusiveGateway", "id": "eg3", "name": "", "conditions": [{"target": "n2", "name": "取消", "expression": "\"${oper_type}\" == \"cancel_white.txt\""}, {"target": "n7", "name": "激活", "expression": "\"${oper_type}\" == \"white.txt\""}]},
  {"type": "ConvergeGateway", "id": "cg3", "name": ""},
  {"type": "EndEvent", "id": "end", "name": ""},
  {"type": "Link", "source": "n1", "target": "eg3"},
  {"type": "Link", "source": "n2", "target": "cg2"},
  {"type": "Link", "source": "eg1", "target": "n3"},
  {"type": "Link", "source": "start", "target": "n4"},
  {"type": "Link", "source": "eg3", "target": "n2"},
  {"type": "Link", "source": "cg2", "target": "cg3"},
  {"type": "Link", "source": "n6", "target": "cg1"},
  {"type": "Link", "source": "eg1", "target": "n1"},
  {"type": "Link", "source": "n7", "target": "cg2"},
  {"type": "Link", "source": "n3", "target": "eg2"},
  {"type": "Link", "source": "n4", "target": "eg1"},
  {"type": "Link", "source": "cg1", "target": "cg3"},
  {"type": "Link", "source": "n5", "target": "cg1"},
  {"type": "Link", "source": "cg3", "target": "end"},
  {"type": "Link", "source": "eg2", "target": "n6"},
  {"type": "Link", "source": "eg3", "target": "n7"},
  {"type": "Link", "source": "eg2", "target": "n5"},
  {"type": "Variable", "key": "${cc_set}", "name": "大区选择", "value": {"datasource": "1", "default": "", "items_text": "<https://xxx",> "type": "1"}, "description": "bkcc_对外大区名称_相同的set会显示成一个大区", "custom_type": "select"},
  {"type": "Variable", "key": "${exec_host}", "name": "执行主机", "value": {"var_filter_module": "test-gamesvr,real-gamesvr", "var_filter_set": "${cc_set}", "var_ip_custom_value": "", "var_ip_manual_value": {"var_manual_module": "all", "var_manual_set": "all", "var_module_name": ""}, "var_ip_method": "manual", "var_ip_select_value": {"var_module": [], "var_module_name": "", "var_set": []}}, "custom_type": "set_module_ip_selector", "show_type": "hide"},
  {"type": "Variable", "key": "${file_account}", "name": "目标账户", "value": "user00", "show_type": "hide", "source_type": "component_inputs"},
  {"type": "Variable", "key": "${file_path}", "name": "目标路径", "value": "/data/home/user00/xxx/bin/xxx_gamesvr", "show_type": "hide", "source_type": "component_inputs"},
  {"type": "Variable", "key": "${job_ip_list}", "name": "目标IP", "value": "${exec_host}", "show_type": "hide", "source_type": "component_inputs"},
  {"type": "Variable", "key": "${job_local_files_info}", "name": "上传白名单txt文件", "value": {"add_files": "", "job_local_files": [], "job_push_multi_local_files_table": [], "job_target_path": "/data/home/user00/backup/whitelist_file_upload_tmp/${log_outputs['cur_time_stamp']}/"}, "description": "1.上传的文本文件后缀名必须为txt\n2.上传文件不要含有特殊字符，如windows结束符，windows换行符等。\n3.一行一个openid", "source_type": "component_inputs"},
  {"type": "Variable", "key": "${local_content}", "name": "请输入open_id", "description": "每一行输入一个open_id", "source_type": "component_inputs"},
  {"type": "Variable", "key": "${local_name}", "name": "生成文件名[后缀]", "value": "${log_outputs['cur_time_stamp']}_${oper_type}", "show_type": "hide", "source_type": "component_inputs"},
  {"type": "Variable", "key": "${log_outputs}", "name": "JOB全局变量", "show_type": "hide", "source_type": "component_outputs"},
  {"type": "Variable", "key": "${oper_type}", "name": "选择操作类型", "value": {"datasource": "0", "default": "", "items_text": "[{\"text\":\"激活\",\"value\":\"white.txt\"},{\"text\":\"取消\",\"value\":\"cancel_white.txt\"}]", "type": "0"}, "description": "选择操作类型", "custom_type": "select"},
  {"type": "Variable", "key": "${plat_id}", "name": "平台ID", "value": {"datasource": "0", "default": "", "items_text": "[{\"text\":\"IOS\",\"value\":\"0\"},{\"text\":\"安卓\",\"value\":\"1\"}]", "type": "1"}, "description": "请选择目标OS平台", "custom_type": "select"},
  {"type": "Variable", "key": "${white_file_upload_type}", "name": "上传白名单的方式", "value": {"datasource": "0", "default": "", "items_text": "[{\"text\":\"【文本框填写】白名单openid\",\"value\":\"【文本框填写】白名单openid\"},{\"text\":\"【本地文件上传】白名单txt文件\",\"value\":\"【本地文件上传】白名单txt文件\"}]", "type": "0"}, "custom_type": "select"}
]
```

```json
  {"type": "Variable", "key": "${input_tempalte_msg}", "name": "发布模板信息", "value": "业务名称: xxx游戏\n是否停机: 是\n是否有更新: 是\n发布范围: 灰度", "custom_type": "textarea"},
  {"type": "Variable", "key": "${task_end_time}", "name": "任务结束时间", "custom_type": "datetime"},
  {"type": "Variable", "key": "${task_start_time}", "name": "任务起始时间", "custom_type": "datetime"},
  {"type": "Variable", "key": "${tgwfilename}", "name": "更新包名及MD5", "value": {"fworks_filelist": [], "fworks_version_nums": [], "fworks_version_type": "server"}, "custom_type": "var_fworks_version_file_selector"},
  {"type": "Variable", "key": "${wid}", "name": "灰度范围", "description": "大区id"},

]
```

```json
  {"type": "StartEvent", "id": "start", "name": ""},
```
